# Page packets — Prosperity

47 pages, 6.625 x 10.25 inches. Made by the writers' room; every packet below is complete on its own.

## How to use these packets

1. **Start with the sheets** below: the character sheet, then the location sheet. Paste each,
   keep the results, and attach them as reference images with every page that names them
   (each page packet says which). Approve one page as your style page and attach that too.
   For a long book, train a LoRA on the approved sheets and pages; references alone drift.
2. **One page at a time.** Paste everything under a page's heading, nothing else. Keep the
   same model and settings for the whole book so the style and the characters stay put.
3. **No text on the pages.** The words are added afterwards, in the room, as a layer over
   your art - so a beautiful model that cannot spell is fine. If the model draws any letters,
   regenerate; do not keep a page with text on it.
4. **Check the page** against its checklist before you keep it. One thing wrong: regenerate
   with the same packet, and only then adjust wording.
5. **Upload each kept page** in the room (Production, Lettering tab) and run the lettering:
   the room draws every balloon and caption where the layout put them, and you download the
   finished, lettered page.

## Rules for every page

- Never render text of any kind: no balloons, captions, sound effects, page numbers,
  titles, chapter names, signatures, watermarks, nameplates or labels.
- Keep the areas each panel lists as clear: uncluttered art (sky, wall, shadow) where a
  balloon will sit, never a face or the thing the panel is about.
- Draw every character exactly as described, every time. The description wins over the
  previous page's picture.
- Panel count, panel shape and reading order are fixed by the map. Do not add, merge or
  reshape panels.

## Character sheet — draw this first

_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one character reference sheet: every character below standing full length in a row, front view, neutral pose, even daylight, plain light background, in the book's style. Same scale for everyone so heights compare. No text, names, labels or captions anywhere on the sheet.

**Style:**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

### Panel 1 — WIDE, EYE ANGLE

**Scene:** Night at a lakeside boathouse in 2099. Older Alex grips a mooring post as TJ steps from the dock and separates mid-stride; Alex's granddaughter stands behind him in a blanket. Leave the upper third of the dock and lake clear for sound lettering.

**Keep these areas clear — the lettering is added afterwards as a separate layer, so draw no words here, just uncluttered art with room for a balloon:**

**Scene:** Close on the granddaughter and older Alex, with a tanker barge and two siphon drones working beneath white lamps across Lake Keel. Keep their faces clear and leave the upper-left lake area open for dialogue.

```text
## Page 1 (right) — Older Alex watches a water theft while TJ breaks himself apart to investigate.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** NIGHT at Lake Keel, 2099. The exhausted mine pit is now a dark lake ringed by terraces, gardens and workshops. Older Alex stands on the boathouse dock with his approximately fifteen-year-old granddaughter. Across the water, two drones lower siphon hoses from a tanker barge.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Grandpa. That’s not a fishing boat.

ALEX: No.

**Panel 2 — MEDIUM.** TJ, a small lopsided robot dog made from mismatched magnetic parts, springs from the dock. In midair, his head, body and legs separate but continue forward.

SFX: KLAK-KLAK-KLAK

GRANDDAUGHTER: Is TJ broken?

ALEX: Not usually.

**Panel 3 — WIDE ON THE LAKE.** TJ’s pieces skim across the water toward the tanker. The siphon draws a bright column from the lake. Dry channels and empty basins scar the far shore.

SFX: WHUMMMMM

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON ALEX AND HIS GRANDDAUGHTER.** A drone beam sweeps over them. She catches his sleeve. Alex watches TJ approach the tanker.

GRANDDAUGHTER: They’re stealing from us.

ALEX: Let’s see what they came to take.

PAGE CHECK: The future lake is restored but still contested. Page turn reveals the thieves.

---
```

## Page 2 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. - **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043.
```text
## Page 4 (left) — TJ makes the theft fail from inside the siphon.

**Panel 1 — CLOSE.** TJ’s head strains between the shutters. His legs reach through a maintenance gap toward the pump assembly.

SFX: KRRRNNK

**Panel 2 — MEDIUM.** On the barge, Nera opens the service panel. A magnetic leg snaps onto the panel’s inner latch.

NERA: Something’s in the housing.

VOSS: Shut it down.

**Panel 3 — WIDE.** The siphon bucks. Water sprays across the deck. The tanker tilts as the pump loses pressure.

SFX: WHUMP—KRAK

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON THE BOATHOUSE.** The granddaughter grips the rail. Alex does not move.

GRANDDAUGHTER: He’s stuck.

ALEX: He knows the difference between stuck and working.

PAGE CHECK: TJ’s plan becomes visible through action, not explanation.

---
```

## Page 5 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

```text
## Page 5 (right) — The thieves lose control, but TJ pays a price.

PAGE CHECK: TJ’s vulnerability is real; the theft is interrupted, not defeated.
```

```text
## Page 6 (left) — TJ returns with evidence instead of victory.

**Panel 1 — UNDERWATER.** TJ’s head searches through dark water. His amber lens catches a small broken piece of drone housing.

**Panel 2 — MEDIUM.** The separated body and legs converge. A leg clamps onto the drone fragment.

SFX: CLK

**Panel 3 — WIDE.** The tanker retreats across the lake with less water than planned. The drones’ beams sweep behind them.

VOSS: Move!

NERA: I’m moving.

**Panel 4 — ON THE DOCK.** Alex kneels as TJ’s head reaches shore, dragging the drone fragment. His body pieces follow unevenly.

GRANDDAUGHTER: He brought something.

ALEX: He usually does.

PAGE CHECK: The prologue’s mystery becomes physical evidence.
```

## Page 7 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. **The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 7 (right) — The question of Alex’s fortune remains open.

**Panel 1 — MEDIUM.** Alex fits TJ’s body together. One leg is missing. The granddaughter holds the drone fragment.

GRANDDAUGHTER: How did you get this lake?

ALEX: I didn’t get it.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON THE FRAGMENT.** Its casing bears a clean commercial mark beside scratches from TJ’s magnetic grip.

GRANDDAUGHTER: They said you made water pay for it.

ALEX: People say things when they want the water.

**Panel 3 — WIDE.** The lake reflects the terraces. The tanker is nearly invisible.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Did you make them pay?

Alex looks across the water.

ALEX: Sometimes the work sends an account.

PAGE CHECK: The language is personal and unresolved, not a technical clue.

---
```

```text
## Page 8 (left) — Alex chooses not to chase the thieves.

**Panel 1 — MEDIUM.** The granddaughter points toward a narrow service boat tied beneath the dock.

GRANDDAUGHTER: We can catch them.

ALEX: Not across open water.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON ALEX’S HAND.** He steadies the damaged TJ against the dock rail.

ALEX: We know what they took. We know what they left.

**Panel 3 — WIDE.** The tanker disappears into darkness. The lake settles around the disturbed water.

GRANDDAUGHTER: That’s enough?

ALEX: Enough to start.

PAGE CHECK: The prologue closes on restraint and evidence, not pursuit.
```

- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. ## Page 10 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **GRANDDAUGHTER** — **Causality** - **Public role:** Small repair robot dog living with older Alex in the 2099 prologue. - **Immediate want:** Investigate and interfere with the drones stealing lake water. - **Deeper need / contradiction:** He is built from scraps but repeatedly acts as if usefulness is worth the risk of coming apart. - **Leverage / capability:** Can separate his head, legs and body segments to enter gaps, scout and manipulate machinery. - **Vulnerability:** Magnetic separation, missing parts, dark
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 10 (left) — The prologue leaves a human question behind the restored landscape.

**Panel 1 — FULL-PAGE IMAGE.** Dawn begins over Lake Keel. The tanker is gone. TJ sits crookedly at Alex’s feet, missing his leg. Alex studies the drone fragment in one hand and the lake in the other direction.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Are you going after them?

ALEX: No.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Why not?

Alex looks toward the old mine walls beneath the terraces.

ALEX: Because chasing people is not the same as making water safe.

PAGE CHECK: Page turn cuts seventy years backward to the same basin before restoration.
```

## Page 12 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **FOREMAN-7** — Foreman-7 is a broad-shouldered human Keel mine foreman in his forties, with dark brown skin, a shaved head, tired dark eyes and a square face mostly obscured by a dust mask and a white-and-amber Alpha-linked supervisory harness. He wears a reinforced charcoal mining coat, plated work trousers, steel-toed boots and a heavy helmet with a small control node at the throat; his silhouette is blocky, official and difficult to read. His signature detail is the white-and-amber node at
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is

```text
## Page 13 (right) — Ada gives Alex part of her ration and refuses to let him turn departure into the only future.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** At the pit rim, Ada sits beside Alex with two battered mugs. The lake is absent below them; only the exhausted mine remains.

ADA: I saved enough for one.

ALEX: There are two mugs.

ADA: I’m generous with containers.

**Panel 2 — MEDIUM.** She hands him a mug. Her thumb rests on the largest rivet of her shoulder strap.

ADA: You’re still leaving.

ALEX: Eventually.

**Panel 3 — CLOSE.** Alex watches the city’s narrow water channels below.

ADA: Keel’s not a sentence.

ALEX: The balance disagrees.

**Panel 4 — WIDE.** They drink while workers move through the crowded lanes below.

PAGE CHECK: Ada is clearly Alex’s closest friend through behavior, not exposition.

---
```

```text
## Page 14 (left) — Adrian’s absence becomes a disputed memory.

**Panel 1 — MEDIUM.** Alex shows Ada an old photograph: Grandmother, Adrian with his face partly folded away, and a much younger Alex.

ALEX: I was two.

ADA: You remember nothing?

ALEX: I remember the watch.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON THE WATCH.** Scratched glass, cream dial, dark display window. The caseback is hidden beneath Alex’s wrist.

ADA: Keel says he gambled.

**Panel 3 — CLOSE ON ADA.**

ADA: Grandmother says he worked.

ALEX: Those aren’t opposites.

**Panel 4 — WIDE.** Wind lifts dust across the pit rim.

ADA: What if both stories are incomplete?

PAGE CHECK: Adrian is neither cleared nor condemned.
```

## Page 15 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **GRANDMOTHER** — Grandmother Phantum is an elderly European-descended woman fully at home in Keel, small and frail but still capable, with soft pale-brown skin, wispy silver hair pinned loosely at the nape, cloudy blue-grey eyes and a lined face that can shift abruptly from confusion to piercing clarity. She wears a faded plum cardigan over a cream blouse, a long charcoal skirt, thick socks and soft house shoes; her silhouette is folded inward around a worn chair. Her signature detail is a smal

## Page 17 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 19 (right) — Adrian’s recording makes the inheritance morally complicated.

**Panel 1 — MEDIUM.** Adrian’s blue projection appears above the workbench, tired and direct.

ADRIAN: Alex. If you’re seeing this, I failed to come back.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON ADRIAN.**

ADRIAN: I borrowed too much. I gambled some of it. I hurt you and Grandmother.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM ON ALEX.** He grips the watch.

ADRIAN (off-panel): The work was shared. The mistakes were mine.

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON THE PROJECTOR.**

ADRIAN: Question everything. Especially me.

PAGE CHECK: Adrian offers no clean defense and no full explanation.

---
```

## Page 20 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ

```text
## Page 20 (left) — Alpha enters as a concrete force before it becomes a mystery.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** In the workshop, Alex’s identity band flashes. Outside, the apartment door’s control node turns amber.

DISPLAY: `MOVEMENT AUTHORIZATION: TEMPORARILY SUSPENDED.`

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON ALEX.**

ALEX: It knows I’m here?

BI11BOT: It knows your band stopped reporting.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** Bi11bot projects the three-city infrastructure spine: water, rail and control lines connecting Keel, Halyard and Oasis.

BI11BOT: Alpha is the network that routes the water, assigns the work and records the permission.

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON ADA.**

ADA: And if its records are wrong?

BI11BOT: Then the wrongness travels efficiently.

PAGE CHECK: Alpha is named and concretely framed as a distributed administrative network, not a person.

---
```

## Page 22 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **FOREMAN-7** — Foreman-7 is a broad-shouldered human Keel mine foreman in his forties, with dark brown skin, a shaved head, tired dark eyes and a square face mostly obscured by a dust mask and a white-and-amber Alpha-linked supervisory harness. He wears a reinforced charcoal mining coat, plated work trousers, steel-toed boots and a heavy helmet with a small control node at the throat; his silhouette is blocky, official and difficult to read. His signature detail is the white-and-amber node at
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 24 (left) — Alex rescues Tomas and accepts an immediate financial penalty.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** In Site 6, a hauling frame has collapsed across Tomas’s leg. Dust fills the work bay. Foreman-7 stands behind an amber-lit control node.

FOREMAN-7: Phantum. Bay six. Return to assigned zone.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON ALEX’S BAND.** Alex steps beyond the marked boundary.

DISPLAY: `LEAVING ASSIGNED ZONE — WAGE FORFEITED: −40 CR.`

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** Alex wedges a salvaged bracket under the frame and cranks a hand tool. Tomas braces against the floor.

TOMAS: You know that costs you.

ALEX: I noticed.

**Panel 4 — WIDE.** The frame lifts enough for Tomas to pull free.

SFX: KRAK

PAGE CHECK: Alex’s boundary is tested by a specific person in danger.

---
```

## Page 25 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **B11** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 25 (right) — Bi11bot admits that a safe plan can still be wrong.

**Panel 1 — MEDIUM.** Alex patches a ruptured ventilation line with the bracket. Tomas coughs dust but stays upright.

BI11BOT: I predicted we could leave him unseen.

ALEX: You were wrong.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON B11.** His right eye ring rotates as he studies the collapsing worksite.

BI11BOT: Yes. What would you do?

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** Foreman-7’s control node flares. Workers turn toward the disturbance.

FOREMAN-7: Obstruction of an authority function is a household offense.

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON TOMAS.** His hand closes around the old key at his belt.

TOMAS: I’d use the door they forgot.

PAGE CHECK: Tomas reveals the unwired entrance through action.

---
```

```text
## Page 26 (left) — Tomas turns a private promise into Alex’s route forward.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** Tomas looks at Bi11bot.

ALEX: You’re coming with us.

TOMAS: No. Someone has to look ordinary.
```

## Page 27 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 29 (right) — Bi11bot chooses the official route and is proven wrong.

**Panel 3 — CLOSE ON ALEX’S HAND.** He touches the newly repaired bolts on the left wall.

ALEX: This route’s been used.

**Panel 4 — MEDIUM.** Bi11bot folds his map projection away.

BI11BOT: I read that wrong.

ALEX: Keep reading.

PAGE CHECK: Judgment shifts from display to evidence.
```

## Page 30 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **B11** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

```text
## Page 30 (left) — The hidden chamber reveals a maintained diversion.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** The left passage opens into old lithium workings. A broad maintained industrial uplink crosses the chamber above a narrow corroded pipe labeled `KEEL RESIDENTIAL`.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON THE PIPE.** The residential line is nearly dry. Its metal is stained white and orange.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** A newer control node glows beside an analog gauge. Adrian’s looping equation is scratched into the housing.

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON ALEX.** He places his palm against the pipe. Water pulses through the broad upper line.

ALEX: Inventory’s moving.

BI11BOT: Toward Halyard.

PAGE CHECK: The water discrepancy becomes a physical route, not an abstract accusation.

---
```

## Page 32 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

```text
## Page 34 (left) — Keel sees the evidence and responds with collective action.

ADA (over public speakers): Same thirty days. Same authorized share.

**Panel 3 — CLOSE ON TOMAS.** At Site 6, Tomas watches the display while Foreman-7’s harness warns him away.

FOREMAN-7: Return to assigned zone.

TOMAS: We’re already in it.

**Panel 4 — WIDE.** Keel workers open boxes, ledgers and old gauges on a communal table.
```

## Page 35 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **LOCK** — Director Cassian Lock is a silver-templed Northern European-descended man in his early sixties, tall and lean with light skin, pale blue-grey eyes, a smooth square jaw and immaculate charcoal hair combed back from a high forehead. He wears a charcoal suit with a platinum collar pin, polished boots and no House crest; his silhouette is long, clean and deliberately neutral, accompanied when necessary by discreet aides rather than visible force. His signature detail is his habit o
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **DIRECTOR** — Director Cassian Lock is a silver-templed Northern European-descended man in his early sixties, tall and lean with light skin, pale blue-grey eyes, a smooth square jaw and immaculate charcoal hair combed back from a high forehead. He wears a charcoal suit with a platinum collar pin, polished boots and no House crest; his silhouette is long, clean and deliberately neutral, accompanied when necessary by discreet aides rather than visible force. His signature detail is his habit o

## Page 37 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **LINA** — Dr. Lina Vey is an Indigenous Andean and mestiza woman in her mid-forties, compact and slightly stooped from years at benches, with medium-brown skin, dark hair threaded with silver and tied low, dark almond-shaped eyes and a narrow, alert face. She wears a maintenance-grade water-and-chemistry House uniform under a patched technical coat, flat work shoes and a stained utility belt; her silhouette is practical and worker-shaped despite her authority. ## Page 40 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **REN** — Ren Okafor is a woman in her late thirties with a broad, muscular build, deep brown skin, a shaved head, dark eyes and a blunt, self-possessed face; her ancestry is not inferred from her surname. She wears a patched Orbital House fabrication suit with scorch marks, a heat-shielded apron, heavy work boots and a clipped respirator; her silhouette is broad-shouldered, grounded and visibly built for outer-rim machinery. Her signature detail is a pair of scorched red gloves tucked i
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 41 (right) — Alex discovers that Adrian’s work belongs to living collaborators.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** The public registry opens four contributor records: Lina, Suri, Jalen and Ren. Each record is marked `RESTRICTED CONTRIBUTION`.

**Panel 2 — MEDIUM.** Dr. Lina Vey meets them beside a chemistry bench.

LINA: Adrian didn’t invent a purifier alone.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** Suri holds a living root cutting in a glass tube.

SURI: Mine dies if the flow is wrong.

**Panel 4 — MEDIUM.** Jalen watches a reflection in his collar mirror while Ren opens a scorched fabrication case.

JALEN: They watch patterns.

REN: Then give them a pattern worth watching.
```

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 42 (left) — The collaborators agree on one immediate objective: a safe, repairable purifier for Keel.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** In a rented Halyard workroom, the four components sit on separate benches. Alex places the cloudy Keel water sample at the center.

ALEX: Keel has water now. It can’t use it.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON LINA.**

LINA: Then we make one unit that treats this water safely.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** Jalen connects his distributed-node shard. Ren fits a mineral-recovery sleeve. Suri positions the living biofilter. Lina adds a membrane cartridge.

**Panel 4 — CLOSE.** The assembled unit sputters at `11% SIMULATED EFFICIENCY.`

SFX: CHUFF

REN: Smaller failure.

PAGE CHECK: The group’s shared goal is explicit through need and work.

---
```

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 44 (left) — The Maker’s Seal turns contribution into visible ownership, and the first honest sale creates a viable alternative.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** A House review team enters. Alex stands beside the purifier with the four collaborators.

ALEX: Lina: membrane chemistry. Suri: living biofilter. Jalen: distributed routing. Ren: mineral recovery and fabrication.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE.** Lina hands Alex the worn bronze Maker’s Seal.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM.** The Seal warms and projects a shared provenance record. No ownership claim disappears; each contributor is named.

LINA: It records who made this.

ALEX: And who carries the work after.

**Panel 4 — WIDE.** At a small Keel shop, the first licensed unit is purchased. A public payment display divides 38 credits:

`12 — ALEX DEBT ACCOUNT`  
`18 — CONTRIBUTOR VENTURE`  
`8 — MAINTENANCE / WARRANTY RESERVE`

PAGE CHECK: Honest business, licensing, profit and responsible ownership all remain legitimate. Page turn reveals the third safeguard.

---

# Chapter 4 — The Broken Covenant
```
