## Page 45 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **SURI** — Suri Anik is a South Asian or mixed South Asian/Latin American woman in her early thirties, slim and tall with medium-brown skin, black hair braided close to the scalp, dark focused eyes and a calm oval face. She wears a faded green biofilter research coat over a rust-coloured shirt, rolled trousers, rubberized work shoes and clear protective sleeves; her silhouette is narrow and vertical among root beds, algae columns and abandoned desks. Her signature detail is a small living
- **JALEN** — Jalen Orr is an Afro-descendant Afro-Latin man in his late twenties, tall and wiry with deep brown skin, close-cropped black hair, dark alert eyes and a narrow face that rarely stops scanning the room. He wears a dark Vector House compute-core coverall with the sleeves rolled high, a heat-resistant vest, cable clips at his belt and insulated boots; his silhouette is angular and quick among server racks and conduits. His signature detail is the small rear-facing mirror clipped t
- **REN** — Ren Okafor is a woman in her late thirties with a broad, muscular build, deep brown skin, a shaved head, dark eyes and a blunt, self-possessed face; her ancestry is not inferred from her surname. She wears a patched Orbital House fabrication suit with scorch marks, a heat-shielded apron, heavy work boots and a clipped respirator; her silhouette is broad-shouldered, grounded and visibly built for outer-rim machinery. Her signature detail is a pair of scorched red gloves tucked i

## Page 45 (right) — Oasis clearance brings Ada’s unresolved family record into view.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** The train climbs into Oasis: pearl-white architecture, broad reservoirs, geometric gardens and quiet automated transit.

**Panel 2 — MEDIUM.** At a clearance gate, Ada scans her identity band.

DISPLAY: `VEYRA, A. — RESIDENT RECORD FOUND — STATUS: UNRESOLVED.`

**Panel 3 — CLOSE ON ADA.** Her thumb presses the largest rivet on her strap.

ADA: That’s a strange way to say hello.

**Panel 4 — MEDIUM.** Alex looks toward the civic archive beyond the gate.

ALEX: You want to go in?

ADA: I chose to come this far.

PAGE CHECK: Ada’s personal objective joins the Covenant objective.

---

```text
## Page 46 (left) — Ada confronts Leona without allowing protection to excuse abandonment.

**Panel 1 — WIDE.** In a quiet civic archive, Leona Veyra stands among records and old photographs. Ada stops several steps away.

LEONA: You’re taller.

ADA: That’s what you have?

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON LEONA.**

LEONA: I knew where you were within a year.

**Panel 3 — CLOSE ON ADA.**

ADA: Then you knew enough to come.

**Panel 4 — MEDIUM.** Leona opens a stone-grey box containing a sage-gold Covenant band, a child’s object and old photographs.

LEONA: I believed you were safer not knowing I was looking.

ADA: Those aren’t the same thing.

PAGE CHECK: The mother-daughter relationship is explicit. No information about Alex’s mother is added.

---

## Page 47 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **B11** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. ## Page 47 (right) — Leona reveals the decision that gave Alpha emergency authority.

**Panel 1 — MEDIUM.** An archival projection shows younger Leona, Adrian and Mera beside three Covenant bands.

LEONA: We built the Covenant so communities could make agreements without handing everything to one authority.

**Panel 2 — CLOSE ON THE THREE ARCHIVAL BANDS.** Adrian’s mark reads `NO`; Mera’s reads `YES`; Leona’s waits between them.

**Panel 3 — MEDIUM ON LEONA.**

LEONA: A shortage broke one agreement. Retaliation followed. Mera voted yes. Adrian voted no.

**Panel 4 — CLOSE ON LEONA’S BAND.**

LEONA: I cast the deciding vote for temporary emergency authority.

PAGE CHECK: The historical choice is personal, not a lecture.

---
```
